- **ext-methodology**: The order of operations for an authorized EXTERNAL penetration test, aligned to PTES (7 phases) and OWASP WSTG. Use whenever planning or driving an external assessment and you need to know what to run before what - pre-engagement/scope, passive intelligence gathering (OSINT), active recon & enumeration, vulnerability analysis / threat modelling, controlled exploitation, post-exploitation, and reporting.
